#### Page 4

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                     Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 1.4 Limits on the Commission and destroyed interrogation records
 ESTABLISHED LIMITATIONS / TARGETED FOLLOW-UP

 The 9/11 Commission itself disclosed that its account of plot planning relied heavily on detainee
 interrogation reports, while it could not interview detainees or their interrogators directly. It
 submitted questions without controlling how they were asked. This materially limits confidence in
 details resting on that reporting alone. It does not erase independently documented travel,
                                                      [5]
 communications, recordings or physical evidence.

 Commission chairs Thomas Kean and Lee Hamilton later described the CIA's failure to disclose
 interrogation tapes as obstruction. They identified the recorded detainees as Abu Zubaydah and
 Abd al-Rahim al-Nashiri. Senate records document destruction in November 2005. These were not
 recordings of Khalid Sheikh Mohammed's account of planning 9/11. Their loss limits scrutiny of
                                                                                  [6][7]
 those interrogations; it does not establish an alternative cause of the attacks.

 The productive follow-up is a proposition-level audit: identify which claims depend on coercive
 interrogation, then identify their independent corroboration, contradictions and remaining gaps.
 Treat uncorroborated intelligence assertions with less confidence than contemporaneous records.

 1.5 Saudi-linked assistance: a serious later inquiry
 LIVE EVIDENTIARY DISPUTE; NO FINAL LIABILITY FINDING

 This is important subsequent evidence, not validation of the film's demolition or
 aircraft-substitution claims. In August 2025 a federal district court found sufficient support for the
 jurisdictional case involving Omar al-Bayoumi, Fahad al-Thumairy and assistance to hijackers to
 reject Saudi Arabia's dismissal motion. Its reasoning addresses evidence of employment, contacts
 and assistance; it is more substantial than a bare allegation, but it is not a final judgment that the
 Saudi state directed or knowingly supported the attacks. [9]

 There is a material counterpoint: the FBI's May 2021 Operation Encore closing memorandum said
 the evidence was insufficient to prosecute a knowing conspiracy and closed the inquiry, with
 reopening possible if new information emerged. Criminal charging and civil jurisdiction apply
 different standards and may consider different records. Neither result should be substituted for the
 other. [8]

 Reporting on the October 7, 2026 appeals hearing described the sovereign-immunity decision as
 pending. This review did not verify a subsequent disposition. Saudi Arabia denies involvement.
 Priority should go to the original exhibits, financial and communication records, witness reliability
 and what changed between the FBI's assessment and the later litigation. [10]

 1.6 Mineta's testimony and military exercises
 AUTHENTIC TESTIMONY; STAND-DOWN INTERPRETATION UNPROVEN

 Norman Mineta really testified about an aide asking Cheney whether orders still stood as an
 aircraft approached. In the same hearing, Mineta said he inferred a shootdown order, rather than
 testifying that he heard an order to let an aircraft strike. His stated timing conflicts with the
 Commission's later placement of Cheney in the shelter conference room shortly before 10:00. The
 Commission acknowledged conflicting arrival evidence. This is a real chronology problem, not a
 documented admission of a stand-down. [32][5]



LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                           Page 4

#### Page 5

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                     Facts, inferences and useful follow-up



 The Commission also records the initial military question about whether the FAA call was real or an
 exercise, followed by clarification that it was real. That exchange supports momentary uncertainty;
 it does not establish that an exercise disabled the response. Evaluation should follow the full
 operational recordings and tasking sequence, while keeping later recollections distinct from
 contemporaneous records. [5]


 2. Buildings and physical evidence
 2.1 Jet fuel, steel and the towers
 MELTING ARGUMENT DOES NOT ESTABLISH DEMOLITION

 Steel need not melt to lose load-bearing capacity. NIST's tower explanation combines aircraft
 damage, damage to fire protection and subsequent fire-driven deformation; it identifies inward
 bowing of perimeter columns and collapse initiation at the impact/fire floors. The argument that jet
 fuel cannot melt structural steel therefore does not rebut the mechanism actually proposed. [13]

 NIST's detailed tower modeling addressed initiation rather than simulating every stage of debris
 movement to the ground. That is a limit on the scope of the model, not an admission that gravity
 could not propagate failure. Claims based on apparent collapse speed or dust jets need
 synchronized measurements of the actual structure; a dust front or first falling exterior panel is not
 automatically a measure of the entire building's descent. [13]

 2.2 WTC 7's free fall
 REAL OBSERVATION / NON-UNIQUE EXPLANATION

 NIST's final report acknowledges approximately 2.25 seconds of gravitational acceleration in the
 descent of the north face, within a longer, three-stage motion. The measurement concerns a
 visible part of the collapse, not proof that the entire failure process occurred in uninterrupted free
 fall. [11]

 That interval implies negligible net support for the descending section. It does not by itself identify
 the mechanism that removed the support. NIST's proposed sequence involves internal failures
 preceding the exterior descent. A demolition explanation must establish its own causal sequence
 and account for the preceding internal motion, timing and physical evidence. [11]

 It is reasonable to demand that a structural explanation account for this measurement. It is not
 reasonable to treat the measurement alone as identification of explosives. That distinction follows
 from mechanics: acceleration describes the resulting forces, not the historical process that
 produced them.




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                           Page 5

#### Page 6

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                   Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 2.3 WTC 7 models, transparency and the Alaska study
 WORTH REPRODUCIBLE TECHNICAL FOLLOW-UP

 NIST attributes WTC 7's initiation to fire-driven floor-system failures around Column 79. A later
 University of Alaska Fairbanks study led by J. Leroy Hulsey disputes fire as the cause and argues
 for near-simultaneous column failures. The university identifies Architects & Engineers for 9/11
 Truth as the funder and offers the model data. This is an identifiable competing engineering study
 whose specific claims can be tested. Funding is relevant context; the calculations must still be
                         [11][24]
 judged on their merits.

 There is a legitimate transparency issue: NIST acknowledges withholding specified model input and
 result files, citing public safety. Independent reproduction is harder when essential inputs are
 unavailable. Neither that restriction nor the existence of a dissenting model establishes demolition.
 [12]


 My assessment: transparency and specific contrary claims warrant an independently reproducible
 comparison; the existence of a dissenting study does not put the explanations on equal evidentiary
 footing. The UAF project summary was examined, but its large final-report download exceeded the
 retrieval tool's limit. I have not independently rerun either model or adjudicated every connection
 calculation. [24]

 2.4 Dust chips and the nanothermite claim
 PUBLISHED CLAIM; DEMOLITION INFERENCE UNESTABLISHED

 Harrit and colleagues published a 2009 paper interpreting red/gray dust chips as unreacted
 thermitic material, based on microscopy, elemental analysis and heating tests. The paper
 documents privately collected samples and acknowledges that heating in air could add energy
 from oxidation of organic material. Thus the observation of a heat-releasing reaction alone does
 not uniquely establish a thermite reaction. [25]

 James Millette's 2012 laboratory report examined visually and elementally similar chips from
 different dust samples and identified an epoxy coating with iron oxide and kaolin, without
 detecting elemental aluminum. It also acknowledged that the exact commercial source was
 undetermined. Because the samples differed, this does not prove that every chip in Harrit's work
 was identical paint; it does show why appearance and elemental ingredients are not a sufficient
 identification. [26]

 The useful test is a blinded, shared-sample replication with documented custody, reference
 coatings and energetic materials, mineral-phase identification and controlled-atmosphere
 calorimetry. Even confirming an energetic material would leave separate questions about its
 amount, origin, placement and causal role. NIST did not perform residue tests for thermite or
 explosives; that omission is real, but is not a positive detection. [13]




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                         Page 6

#### Page 7

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                      Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 2.5 FEMA's unusual steel and reports of molten material
 REAL OBSERVATIONS WITH LIMITED DIAGNOSTIC VALUE

 FEMA Appendix C documented unusual oxidation, sulfidation and localized intergranular melting in
 two steel samples. It explicitly left the sulfur source, corrosion rate and whether the damage
 developed before collapse or during prolonged heating afterward unresolved. Calling this
 observation fictional would be wrong. Calling it proof of thermate would also go beyond the report.
 [27]


 The first sample's building attribution was tentative in the introduction, and its original structural
 location was unknown. That limits what it can demonstrate about collapse initiation. The authors
 called for further study. A useful follow-up would trace the retained specimens, subsequent
                                                             [27]
 analyses and realistic sulfur-bearing exposure conditions.

 Reports or images of glowing or flowing material require composition, location and timing before
 they can establish molten structural steel or a specific incendiary. The same standard applies to
 confident alternative identifications: an untested image cannot conclusively identify an alloy.
 Evidence from the debris pile after collapse must also be distinguished from evidence of the
 initiating event.

 2.6 Explosions, witnesses and 'pull it'
 TESTIMONY DESERVES CONTEXT; A DEVICE IS NOT IDENTIFIED

 The examined film transcript includes WTC 7 witnesses describing explosions and damage during
 their escape. Those experiences deserve to be taken seriously. The description of an explosion
 does not, by itself, identify a planted explosive: establishing a source requires reliable timing,
 location, recordings and physical evidence. This review did not independently synchronize each
 witness account, including Barry Jennings's, against the tower collapses and other events. It
 therefore does not claim every account has been individually explained. [2]

 Larry Silverstein's phrase "pull it" is real. A BBC Trust editorial record reports his subsequent
 explanation that he meant withdrawing firefighters. That is evidence of his stated meaning, not
 independent proof of every operational detail. I did not acquire the unedited original interview. The
 phrase alone does not establish that demolition equipment had been installed or that he ordered
 its activation. The full interview and contemporaneous fire-command records are the appropriate
 evidence for the narrower dispute. [28]


 3. Aircraft, calls and identities
 3.1 The Pentagon aircraft
 AIRCRAFT-SUBSTITUTION CLAIM CONTRADICTED BY COMBINED EVIDENCE

 The aircraft claim must be assessed against positive evidence. The NTSB flight-path study
 combined the recovered Flight 77 flight-data recorder with several radar sources and
 reconstructed its approach to the Pentagon. The ASCE building-performance investigation,
 summarized by NIST, examined extensive structural damage, building plans, aircraft information
 and witness accounts. These sources describe a damaging aircraft impact, rather than inferring
 one merely from the government's announcement. [33][34]



LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                            Page 7

#### Page 8

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                   Facts, inferences and useful follow-up



 The Defense Department's published Pentagon history also records recovered aircraft fragments
 and both flight recorders; the flight-data recorder yielded useful information, while the cockpit
 voice recorder did not. A claim that there was no aircraft wreckage or no recovered flight recorder
 is contradicted by those records. [35]

 An unclear security-camera image cannot by itself distinguish a missile from a large aircraft. The
 stronger substitution claim needs to explain the radar, recorder provenance, wreckage and
 damage together. The reviewed evidence supports Flight 77 striking the Pentagon. This is an
 assessment of converging records, not a new independent authentication of every recovered
 component.

 3.2 Flight 93 and a shootdown or substitute landing
 CLAIM NOT SUPPORTED BY THE CRASH RECORD

 The crash record includes recovered aircraft wreckage and both flight recorders. The Justice
 Department's artifact collection identifies a fuselage fragment carrying cockpit number 5491 as
 evidence used to identify the aircraft. The published cockpit-voice transcript records a struggle and
 commands to put the aircraft down. Together these are substantial positive evidence against a
 substituted landing and are consistent with the hijackers crashing the aircraft during the passenger
 assault. [36][37][38]

 There was dispersed debris: the National Park Service distinguishes light material found as far
 away as New Baltimore from larger wreckage at the crash site. It also reports no evidence of
 explosives there. Distant paper is not equivalent to a heavy component separating in flight.
 Conversely, dismissing every distant-debris report as a road-distance error would be inaccurate.
 Material, weight, location, timing and wind conditions matter. [36]

 This review read the released transcript, not the sealed original cockpit audio. A shootdown
 hypothesis would need evidence such as weapon fragments, a matching engagement record or a
 flight-data discontinuity attributable to a weapon. The reviewed records do not provide that
 evidence. [50]

 3.3 Phone calls and voice fabrication
 TECHNOLOGICAL POSSIBILITY IS NOT EVENT EVIDENCE

 The claim that airborne phone calls were impossible depends heavily on confusing ordinary mobile
 phones with the aircraft's installed Airfones. The National Park Service's source-based Flight 93
 record identifies 35 Airfone calls among 37 calls, with billing evidence introduced at the Moussaoui
 trial. It distinguishes actual recordings from witnesses' later recollections of conversations. [30]

 This materially undermines a wholesale fabricated-calls theory. Voice imitation being technically
 possible does not establish that these calls were fabricated. A persuasive challenge would have to
 explain the billing records, recipient accounts and contemporaneous reporting together. Particular
 disputes about one call's device, duration or wording should be evaluated individually; they cannot
 erase the rest of the record by association.




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                         Page 8

#### Page 9

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                    Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 3.4 Alleged surviving hijackers
 EARLY IDENTITY REPORTS DO NOT ESTABLISH SURVIVAL

 The FBI's first public list was issued on September 14, 2001 with uneven identification details,
 aliases and alternative dates of birth. Early reporting should not be treated as a flawless
 identification record. But finding a living person with the same or a similar name does not establish
                                                                                             [48]
 that the person later identified by photograph, travel history and other evidence survived.

 Later FBI forensic reporting describes DNA profiles developed for several hijackers from crash-site
 and hotel-room items; it does not claim that all nineteen were identified by DNA. The evidentiary
                                                    [51]
 record therefore extends beyond a list of names.

 This review did not complete a person-by-person audit of every alleged survivor or obtain the
 original BBC correction page. Accordingly, its conclusion is bounded: the early same-name reports
 do not establish survival of the identified attackers. Any serious renewed claim should match the
 person across photographs, birth details, documents and movements, then address the later
 identification evidence.


 4. Money and foreknowledge
 4.1 Options trades before the attacks
 REAL STATISTICAL QUESTION; FOREKNOWLEDGE NOT ESTABLISHED

 There was a legitimate reason to investigate. Allen Poteshman's 2006 peer-reviewed study found
 unusually high long-put buying before the attacks, consistent with informed trading; its
 volume-ratio measures did not yield the same conclusion. A statistical signal is therefore not
 imaginary, but its interpretation depends on the measure and benchmark. The study did not
 identify perpetrators or establish the information behind a particular trade. [39]

 The SEC reported examining more than 9.5 million transactions and finding no evidence of
 attack-foreknowledge trading. The Commission's financing monograph gives a concrete example:
 an adviser responsible for 95% of United's September 6 put volume also bought substantial
 American shares on September 10 and described a broader airline strategy. Investigators
 considered that fuller portfolio as well as the trader's identity. [40][41]

 The published account-level findings weigh against attack foreknowledge. Independent scrutiny
 would be stronger if flagged trades could be reconciled with the released investigative workpapers,
 beneficial ownership, offsetting positions and contemporaneously documented reasons. An
 unusual pattern should not be described as proof of foreknowledge.

 4.2 The Pentagon's $2.3 trillion
 REAL ACCOUNTING FAILURE; NOT EVIDENCE THAT $2.3 TRILLION VANISHED

 The $2.3 trillion figure referred to accounting entries unsupported by adequate documentation or
 audit trails, not an identified $2.3 trillion cash balance that vanished. A May 9, 2000 congressional
 hearing already discussed the figure in reviewing fiscal 1999 Defense Department accounts. The
 problem therefore predates September 10, 2001 and was already public. [42]




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                          Page 9

#### Page 10

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                   Facts, inferences and useful follow-up



 Unsupported adjustments are a severe control and audit problem: they can conceal errors and
 make misuse harder to detect. Their gross total is not itself a calculation of stolen money, since
 accounting entries can move, reconcile or reclassify the same underlying amounts. Showing that a
 subsequent attack destroyed the evidence of a particular theft would require identifying that theft
 and the relevant records; the coincidence of a speech and the attack does not do so.

 4.3 Silverstein's insurance
 INSURANCE AND LITIGATION ARE REAL; MOTIVE ALONE PROVES NOTHING

 The insurance and ensuing litigation were real. The Second Circuit's 2003 opinion records that the
 WTC lease required insurance, that roughly $3.5 billion of coverage per occurrence was arranged,
 and that actual property loss substantially exceeded that amount. The litigation concerned how
                                                                        [45]
 differing policy and binder language treated the two aircraft impacts.

 This gives the purchase and dispute an ordinary contractual context. It does not establish a
 deliberately engineered windfall. A gross insurance recovery is not a calculation of net profit:
 acquisition costs, property losses, debt and rebuilding obligations must be considered. Evidence of
 foreknowledge would require a documented connection to the attack, beyond buying required
 insurance and arguing for coverage after the loss.

 4.4 Operation Northwoods and PNAC
 AUTHENTIC CONTEXT; NO DOCUMENTED BRIDGE TO EXECUTION OF 9/11

 Operation Northwoods was real. The March 1962 Joint Chiefs memorandum proposed
 manufactured pretexts for intervention in Cuba, including staged attacks and an aircraft deception.
 It is direct evidence that senior officials proposed such methods. The document is a planning
 submission, not evidence that its proposals were executed on September 11, 2001. [43]

 PNAC's 2000 report also really used the phrase 'new Pearl Harbor' while discussing how a
 catastrophe could accelerate military transformation. The surrounding passage concerns
 procurement, institutional change and the pace of transformation. It supplies evidence of a
 strategic worldview, not an operational instruction to carry out 9/11. [44]

 Historical precedent and potential political benefit can inform hypotheses. Establishing this
 particular operation requires a documented bridge: people, instructions, resources,
 implementation and corroborating records. These texts do not supply that bridge.


 5. Specific claims that change under scrutiny
 5.1 Security was reduced and bomb dogs were removed
 NARROW REPORT IS REAL; BROADER ABSENCE CLAIM FAILS

 A contemporary Newsday report quoted security guard Daria Coard saying that extended shifts
 following telephone threats had ended and bomb-sniffing dogs had been removed the preceding
 Thursday. The original newspaper archive was not acquired; its republished text was examined.
 This supports a reported reduction in an extra security deployment, without establishing every
 entrance, shift or canine assignment. [14]




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                        Page 10

#### Page 11

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                      Facts, inferences and useful follow-up



 The 9/11 Memorial Museum's accession record documents bomb-detection dog Sirius with Officer
 David Lim in the South Tower basement that morning. Therefore a claim that there were no bomb
 dogs at the WTC is false. The useful remaining questions concern the actual staffing changes, their
 authorization and the threat assessment behind them. Rosters and canine deployment logs could
 resolve those questions; the article alone cannot. [15]

 5.2 The BBC reported WTC 7's collapse prematurely
 REAL REPORTING ERROR; FOREKNOWLEDGE INFERENCE UNSUPPORTED

 The premature report is real. Reporting tracing the BBC's explanation identifies an erroneous
 Reuters account, subsequently withdrawn, amid warnings that the building might collapse. The
 original BBC blog pages were inaccessible in this review, and the original Reuters dispatch and
 withdrawal were not independently acquired. That limits verification of the precise transmission
        [23]
 chain.

 An early report of an anticipated event is compatible with a newsroom converting a warning into a
 statement that it has happened. To establish advance operational knowledge, the necessary
 evidence would be an authenticated source chain containing information unavailable from public
 warnings. Repeatedly replaying the same error does not provide that evidence.

 5.3 Willie Brown and John Ashcroft received special warnings
 NARROW FACTS; SPECIFIC-ATTACK FOREKNOWLEDGE NOT SHOWN

 The contemporaneous Willie Brown story describes a routine warning from airport security that did
 not deter his planned trip. It does not identify Condoleezza Rice as the caller or document warning
 of the particular attack. A claim attributing the call to Rice needs a separate original record. [16]

 Ashcroft testified that official travel on government aircraft followed personal-protection advice,
 while he continued personal commercial travel, including on September 3. That is his sworn
 account, not an independently reconstructed travel ledger. The available testimony does not
 establish that he stopped commercial travel because he knew about 9/11. His underlying threat
 assessment and travel records would be the appropriate tests. [17]

 5.4 A CIA officer met bin Laden at a Dubai hospital
 PUBLISHED ALLEGATION; NOT VERIFIED

 Contemporary reporting confirms that Le Figaro published the allegation, relying on an anonymous
 source. It also records denials from the CIA and the hospital. The existence of a newspaper report
 establishes that the allegation circulated; it does not establish the meeting. No authenticated
 medical, travel or meeting record was found in this review. [21][22]

 This merits reconsideration if a source supplies a verifiable contemporaneous document or an
 identifiable firsthand witness. Without that, it should receive little evidentiary weight. A denial
 alone would not settle the matter, but an uncorroborated accusation does not settle it either.

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                    Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 5.5 Gold disappeared from the WTC vaults
 THE LARGE-THEFT NARRATIVE LACKS AN AUTHENTICATED INVENTORY

 Reuters reported in November 2001 that the NYBOT and ScotiaMocatta announced recovery and
 relocation of all metals in their 4 WTC depository to an approved Brinks facility, with the metal
 available for futures delivery. That is a named institutional recovery claim, not a new independent
                                         [20]
 audit of every possible WTC holding.

 The film's much larger gold figure is not established by a verified pre-attack inventory. Comparing
 an unsupported total with a reported recovery amount cannot demonstrate a theft. A serious
 investigation would require vault-specific ownership and bar lists, delivery warrants, carrier records
 and reconciled quantities. A photograph of recovered metal in a vehicle does not supply the
                    [3]
 missing inventory.

 5.6 WTC 7's destruction erased SEC investigations
 PHYSICAL LOSSES WERE REAL; TOTAL ERASURE IS CONTRADICTED

 The SEC lost its regional office. But Chairman Harvey Pitt told Congress on September 20, 2001
 that its electronically stored documents had been recovered and that cases were being reviewed
 and reconstructed. The SEC inspector general also described evidence recovery from backups and
 original sources, while acknowledging disruption. [18][19]

 These records contradict the suggestion that destruction of the building necessarily eliminated all
 records of its investigations. They do not prove that every paper file or evidentiary item was
 recovered. A worthwhile narrower question is which cases suffered irrecoverable losses and
 whether case outcomes changed for that reason. That requires case-level loss and reconstruction
 records.


 6. The most useful next research
 These are ranked by evidentiary value and the chance that identifiable records could improve the
 answer. They are recommendations for further research, not claims that those records have
 already been obtained.

 Priority 1: Reconstruct warning and response accountability
 Build one timeline from dated CIA/FBI dissemination records, FAA/NORAD recordings, interview
 dates, the successive official narratives and inspector-general findings. For each discrepancy
 record who knew what, at what time, and which underlying record supports it. This distinguishes
 operational mistakes, later inaccurate recollections and knowing misstatements. The most
 consequential discovery would be a contemporaneous instruction or documented suppression that
 survives comparison with independent records.




LOOSE CHANGE | EVIDENCE REVIEW | NOVEMBER 2026                                                         Page 12

#### Page 13

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                   Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 Priority 2: Examine the Saudi assistance evidence at exhibit level
 Start with the 2025 court decision and exhibits concerning Bayoumi, Thumairy, employment and
 payments, then compare them with the 2021 Encore closing assessment and later releases.
 Separate an actual contact or payment from an inference about its purpose. Identify what each
 witness personally observed. Follow the appeal for procedural status without treating a
                                                         [8][9][10]
 jurisdictional decision as a verdict on responsibility.

 Priority 3: Test specific WTC 7 claims with reproducible models
 Use the same documented geometry, connection details, fire assumptions and video time base to
 compare competing models. Publish inputs, sensitivity ranges, failure criteria and predictions
 before matching the complete observed sequence. Ask which conclusion changes when an
 uncertain input changes, and whether either model predicts observations it was not fitted to. A
 model that reproduces descent after prescribed support removal has not yet explained what
 removed those supports.

 Request a precise inventory of withheld NIST files and pursue technically useful access
 arrangements where appropriate. NIST acknowledges withholding some model files on
 public-safety grounds. That is a real reproducibility issue; withholding alone says nothing about
 whether the files would favor the official or an alternative explanation. [12]

 Priority 4: Audit evidence preservation and interrogation dependence
 For steel, documents and recordings, reconstruct custody, retention and disposal decisions and
 identify the questions each loss prevents answering. For plot-planning claims, map
 interrogation-derived assertions to genuinely independent corroboration. This produces a
 confidence assessment for individual propositions. [4][5][6][31]

 Priority 5: Reconcile trading studies with traced transactions
 Replicate statistical results with predeclared time windows, comparable control periods,
 corrections for multiple searches and full strategy-level positions. Then match flagged trades,
 where lawful records permit, to ownership, offsets and contemporaneous reasons. An unusual
 market pattern, evidence of advance knowledge, and participation in planning the attacks are
 three different propositions. Identity and transaction documentation are the discriminating
 evidence.

 Lower priority without new evidence
 Recycling ambiguous phrases, compressed news clips, alleged same-name survivors, low-quality
 impact frames or unsupported inventory figures is unlikely to advance the inquiry. These topics
 should move up the queue only when new authenticated records address the specific evidentiary
 gap.

#### Page 14

PUBLIC-SOURCE RESEARCH                                                   Facts, inferences and useful follow-up




 Limits and overall assessment
 What this review establishes
 There is a substantial documented basis for criticizing preparedness, information sharing, some
 official representations and evidence preservation. There are also technical uncertainties that
 justify transparent review. Those conclusions do not depend on accepting the film's most dramatic
 claims.

 The aircraft and demolition allegations need positive, coherent evidence of their own. A genuine
 failure in one investigation cannot fill an evidentiary gap in another subject. The strongest overall
 reading of the reviewed record remains an al-Qaeda attack amid serious failures of prevention and
 response, followed by documented shortcomings in investigation and accountability. Claims of an
                                                                [33][37][46][52]
 alternative execution mechanism are not established here.

 This review did not rerun engineering models, authenticate original broadcast tapes or examine
 physical samples. It did not independently audit every financial transaction, assess every released
 intelligence file or watch every edition frame by frame. Some primary pages were inaccessible, and
 those limitations are stated at the affected claims. Current legal status is based on the latest
 material located, not a guarantee that no later docket entry exists.

 Give credence to the supported facts, calibrate confidence in explanations, and pursue records
 capable of distinguishing competing accounts. That approach preserves the legitimate questions
 without granting unsupported conclusions more weight than their evidence warrants.

# Preliminary research: source assessment and follow-ups

**Working research, November 5, 2026.** The user's [supplied text](SUPPLIED-RESEARCH.txt) is preserved byte-for-byte as received, including its unresolved citation artifacts. SHA-256: `1fd516af948ef648ccd6e3655be265109462f57fff38d8ce6140cf3e5f1a9259`. It is a secondary research submission, not a raw FBI exhibit or established facts bank. This note checks selected citations, not every proposition in the submission. The [prompts](DEEP-RESEARCH-PROMPTS.md) request further source recovery and balanced tests. Current request status must be checked against the [case tracker](/Users/admin/docs/911/correspondence/fbi-1761252-000-tracker.md) and original receipts.

## Assessment

The submission identifies useful, concrete records trails. It does **not** establish that UMS or Max Movers accessed the WTC for a demolition operation, planted explosives, worked for Mossad, coordinated with the hijackers or Saudi intermediaries, or arranged the attacks to produce an Iraq war. Those are distinct propositions requiring distinct evidence. An intelligence affiliation, if established, would not alone establish a demolition role or advance knowledge.

The most efficient follow-ups seek identifiable records across the missing links: (1) dated building access and moving-job records, (2) the conditional Exxon video match and final FBI assessment, and (3) the original Classic/Miami lead and seized notebook entry. Separately, test the alleged CIA–Saudi liaison and the physical-evidence claims without treating a result in either area as attribution to UMS.

## Claim ledger and source checks

| Claim or lead | Evidence status and important limitation | Follow-up / potentially contrary result |
|---|---|---|
| **Bayoumi / Saudi intelligence.** The submission cites a June 14, 2017 FBI memorandum, `EO14040-002638–002639`, reproduced in a [Senate document](https://www.hsgac.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023-09-10-Blumenthal-and-Johnson-Letter-to-DOJ-and-FBI-re-Sept.-11.pdf). | The linked 17-page Senate PDF was opened, but its embedded scan was not independently transcribed in this pass. A [Senate hearing search result](https://www.hsgac.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/CHRG-118shrg53703.pdf) separately identifies the memorandum and reports a paid GIP cooptee; the underlying sources and redactions need direct page-image review. Affiliation is not evidence of knowing assistance to an attack. | Inspect the actual 2017 pages, source attribution and subsequent FBI and court assessments; seek underlying source reporting and contemporaneous records. |
| **Saudi civil litigation vs. FBI closure.** | The submission identifies the [2025 jurisdictional opinion](https://wp.nysd.uscourts.gov/sites/default/files/2025-08/03-md-01570%2011182.pdf) and [2021 FBI closure assessment](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11-attacks-investigation-and-related-materials/9-11-material-released-in-response-to-executive-order-14040/documents-responsive-to-executive-order-14040-section-2-b-ii-part-01-of-01/at_download/file); these documents were not fully re-audited in this pass. The different procedural and investigative standards matter: neither a jurisdictional inference nor a prosecution assessment is a final trial finding. | Compare exact wording, sources, evidentiary standards and dates, including rejected interpretations and contrary evidence. |
| **Bayoumi–Awlaki chronology.** | The supplied account cites 2026 reporting about footage and a 2021 denial. But a [public 2024 plaintiffs' filing](https://www.motleyrice.com/sites/default/files/documents/AT_HR/9-11/20240802%20Redacted%20public%20averment.pdf), PDF pp. 241–242, already described the January 1998 video, paintball footage and the alleged contradiction; it cites video clips and screenshots and reports a 2003 Commission interview in which Bayoumi acknowledged contact. This is an attributed plaintiffs' account, not independent authentication of each clip or a finding of guilt. The date on which the underlying video itself became publicly accessible remains to be established. | Obtain the original clips, release/docket history, 2021 deposition, 2003 interview record and identification/custody evidence. Distinguish a newly public video from a newly discovered relationship. |
| **CIA–Saudi recruitment allegation.** | The cited [Canestraro declaration](https://www.floridabulldog.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Canestraro-Declaration-dated-20-July-2021.pdf) opened as an image-only PDF; the cited pages were not visually rechecked in this pass. The user's account attributes detailed recruitment and CIA-file allegations to a confidential source. Treat those allegations as unverified until the pages and provenance are inspected; even a faithful quotation would establish an allegation, not its truth. | Retrieve pp. 20–21 and the [military-commission motion](https://www.mc.mil/Portals/0/pdfs/KSM2/KSM%20II%20%28AE827%28AAA%20WBA%29%29_Part1.pdf), original witnesses and operational documents; test competing accounts. No UMS connection is identified by this allegation. |
| **January 2000 CIA hold.** | The [DOJ inspector general](https://oig.justice.gov/sites/default/files/archive/special/s0606/chapter5.htm) records that a CIA deputy chief directed a draft report be held and that it was not disseminated to the FBI; the report also says neither inspector general found corroboration for a cable's claim that the travel documents had already been shared. That supports a particular hold, not its purpose, a wider agreement or advance knowledge of the attacks. | Reconstruct original draft, hold note, follow-up, cable, interviews and recipients; compare agency responses and evidence of error, source protection or intentional obstruction. |
| **FBI interview count addendum.** | The supplied text cites a September 2021 addendum correcting an interview-count statement. The relevant page was not independently inspected here. | Find the exact addendum and what universe and period its count covers; do not generalize it to every 9/11 investigation. |
| **UMS affiliation / final findings.** | The [Forward account](https://forward.com/news/325698/spy-rumors-fly-on-gusts-of-truth/) is attributed reporting about a claimed FBI assessment; this pass did not independently obtain the assessment or verify the reporting's sources. Distinguish affiliation, surveillance target, knowledge and attack participation. Existing work records an investigator no-foreknowledge conclusion; inspect the original and scope. Redaction of [Serial 459](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20459%20---%20Section%201%20%281037765%29.pdf) cannot be read as confirming any allegation. | Seek final headquarters inquiry, supporting records, database hits, interviews and all positive and negative results. Compare original closure language with the reporter's account. |
| **Exxon video, Serial 325.** | The [October 17, 2001 communication](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20325%20---%20Section%201%20%281037758%29.pdf), printed p. 4, was checked: it reports a *possible* white-van match and expressly makes the possible pre-impact inference conditional on identification. It does not establish the match, validate the recorded time, identify occupants or give a final resolution. | Seek original recording, time calibration, comparisons and lab work, eyewitness interviews and final findings. A mismatch, inaccurate clock or after-impact sighting would weaken the advance-positioning hypothesis. |
| **Classic / Miami.** | The [prior local review](../revisions/2026-11-05-classic-source-user-check/REVIEW.md) records a redacted September 15 Miami belief, a September 23 notebook lead and an uncertain 24-page fax association. It explicitly notes that its prior pass did not freshly inspect the underlying page images. None identifies the alleged hijacker or independently confirms a moving job. Multiple FBI summaries may repeat the same lead. | Recover the original Miami lead, employee interviews, notebook and customer records. A mistaken lead, unrelated company or no match would narrow the connection. The prior submitted Classic request, portal confirmation `1197336`, seeks these materials; its receipt is not a production. |
| **White House archival locator.** | The [NARA notification](https://www.archives.gov/files/foia/pra-notifications/pdf/bush43/rn-plgwb-2026-023.pdf), PDF p. 16, identifies case `2025-0218-F`: 13 assets processed, 10 withheld in full, 2 in part and 1 proposed open in full. The request mixes UMS with unrelated terms; the notice does not establish which records concern UMS or that proposed opening occurred. | Locate actual released assets, descriptions and search-term matches, and determine access or appeal status. No UMS link should be inferred from the case title. |
| **WTC physical evidence.** | The submission describes [FEMA Appendix C](https://www.fema.gov/pdf/library/fema403_apc.pdf), [Harrit et al.](https://benthamopenarchives.com/abstract.php?ArticleCode=TOCPJ-2-7), [Millette](https://aneta.org/911experiments_com/Millette/paper/index.htm) and [UAF](https://ine.uaf.edu/wtc7); those analyses were not fully audited in this pass. The studies and specimens are different; UAF's project concerns WTC 7, not proof of the towers' collapse mechanism or the identity of a person. The [NIST towers FAQ](https://www.nist.gov/world-trade-center-investigation/study-faqs/wtc-towers-investigation) was checked and says NIST did not test tower steel for explosives or thermite residue. The separate [WTC 7 FAQ](https://www.nist.gov/world-trade-center-investigation/study-faqs/wtc-7-investigation) describes a 2.25-second interval of north-face free fall within a longer movement and explains its conclusion against a blast. Neither a lack of residue testing nor the interval proves demolition. | Trace sample origins, custody, testing methods, alternative materials and same-specimen replication; compare models, observations, parameters and uncertainties, with WTC 7 kept separate from the towers. |
| **Iraq policy and contracting.** | The submission links pre-9/11 policy papers and post-attack notes, but those documents were not independently transcribed here. The [GAO report](https://www.gao.gov/assets/a242676.html) was checked: it found the November 2002 KBR oil-planning order outside the existing contract's scope, a point contested by DOD. Assess the later award separately on its own facts. Post-attack policy and business opportunity are not proof of advance involvement. | Inspect dated originals, decision authority, alternatives and independent evidence of contemporaneous communications. Seek evidence that could disconfirm preplanned attack coordination as well as evidence for it. |

## Status and boundaries

Use the existing [developing outline](../DEVELOPING-THEORY.md) alongside this assessment; no unsupported claim has been promoted to a fact, exhibit, pleading or final theory. These leads overlap prior work, so they are not counted as independent evidence merely because they appear again. The [research prompts](DEEP-RESEARCH-PROMPTS.md) are ready to run; they do not authorize contacting people, filing requests, sending messages, granting fees or publishing material. Check the live tracker before changing or duplicating any FOIA request.

**There is substantial corroboration for several components of your theory—particularly Saudi-intelligence connections, deliberately interrupted information-sharing, and efforts to connect 9/11 to Iraq.** The strongest additions are below. The central gap in your map—evidence connecting UMS personnel to WTC access, installation of explosives, and coordination with the hijackings—remains unfilled by this search. Pasted text(20261106-024554)

This review covers public material located through **November 5, 2026**. I distinguish original government records, allegations in litigation, and reporting based on sources whose underlying evidence is not public.

## 1. Saudi assistance: a more concrete intelligence connection

**A June 14, 2017 FBI memorandum expressly identifies Omar al-Bayoumi as a paid Saudi intelligence collaborator.** Titled *“Albayoumi / GIP Cooptee,”* it states that he received a monthly stipend through Ambassador Prince Bandar and forwarded information about the Saudi community for Saudi General Intelligence Presidency purposes. The memo says this information confirmed intelligence-affiliation allegations that had remained unconfirmed when the 9/11 Commission reported. The document is reproduced in an official Senate records request, with identifiers **EO14040-002638–002639**. [HSGAC](https://www.hsgac.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023-09-10-Blumenthal-and-Johnson-Letter-to-DOJ-and-FBI-re-Sept.-11.pdf)

That supports a more precise proposition than “a Saudi-connected man helped hijackers”: **the FBI subsequently recorded an intelligence relationship involving the same intermediary.** The source and some supporting details remain redacted, so the public cannot independently audit the underlying intelligence. The memorandum does not itself establish that his assistance was knowingly directed toward the attacks. [HSGAC](https://www.hsgac.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023-09-10-Blumenthal-and-Johnson-Letter-to-DOJ-and-FBI-re-Sept.-11.pdf)

The **August 28, 2025 federal court decision** adds evidentiary weight. Judge George Daniels found sufficient evidence at the jurisdictional stage to support inferences of knowing or deliberately indifferent assistance and government coordination. Among the evidence discussed was Bayoumi’s handwritten aircraft diagram and calculations. Importantly, the judge did not accept every plaintiffs’ interpretation: he rejected treating the apartment gathering as a demonstrated terrorist welcome party or the Capitol video as proven reconnaissance on the showing presented. This was a substantive assessment of evidence, but **not a final liability finding**. [U.S District Court](https://wp.nysd.uscourts.gov/sites/default/files/2025-08/03-md-01570%2011182.pdf)

**A significant subsequent development is the BBC’s September 7, 2026 investigation.** It reported newly public footage showing Bayoumi embracing Anwar al-Awlaki in January 1998 and participating in paintball outings involving him—contradicting Bayoumi’s 2021 testimony that they had not met. According to the BBC, the footage entered the public docket on September 4, 2026. That is corroboration of an actual relationship, rather than a geographical coincidence or telephone-directory association. It does not, by itself, establish knowledge of the eventual attack plan. [AOL.com](https://www.aol.com/articles/videos-man-9-11-lawsuit-050038000.html)

The BBC also interviewed former investigators who described unsuccessful attempts to obtain British-seized evidence and permission to interview Saudi officials. Those accounts support investigating **specific restrictions on the investigation**, although the public reporting does not establish why those restrictions occurred. [AOL.com](https://www.aol.com/articles/videos-man-9-11-lawsuit-050038000.html)

The principal contrary official finding remains the FBI’s **May 27, 2021 closure assessment**, which concluded that insufficient evidence existed to prosecute Bayoumi, Thumairy, and Jarrah for knowingly conspiring to assist the attacks. That conclusion and the later jurisdictional ruling answer different questions under different standards; neither should be substituted for the other. [FBI](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11-attacks-investigation-and-related-materials/9-11-material-released-in-response-to-executive-order-14040/documents-responsive-to-executive-order-14040-section-2-b-ii-part-01-of-01/at_download/file)

## 2. The Saudi–CIA liaison hypothesis has a specific evidentiary lead

Your map describes a liaison channel as an inference to test. There is a more specific allegation worth adding: **Donald Canestraro’s July 20, 2021 declaration**, associated with military-commission discovery motion **AE827 (AAA WBA)**. The official motion expressly seeks discovery concerning Bayoumi and a possible recruitment operation. [mc.mil](https://www.mc.mil/Portals/0/pdfs/KSM2/KSM%20II%20%28AE827%28AAA%20WBA%29%29_Part1.pdf)

Canestraro, a defense investigator, reports interviews with former intelligence and law-enforcement personnel. On printed pages **20–21**, a confidential source designated **CS-23**, described as a former FBI special agent, alleges that:

- The CIA maintained operational files on Bayoumi despite telling FBI personnel it had no files.
- Bayoumi was instructed through Thumairy to try to recruit Hazmi and Mihdhar.
- The proposed recruitment was CIA-directed through Saudi intelligence liaison, and subsequent investigation was suppressed.

These are unusually specific allegations concerning an operational relationship and a claimed documentary trail. But **the declaration establishes that Canestraro reported receiving these statements—not that their underlying claims have been independently proved**. The source is unnamed publicly, and the operational files, tasking instructions, and purported witnesses are not supplied in those passages. [Florida Bulldog](https://www.floridabulldog.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Canestraro-Declaration-dated-20-July-2021.pdf)

This makes the recruitment hypothesis more than an inference from suspicious coincidences. It supplies a concrete allegation that can be tested against records. It still does not connect such a recruitment attempt to UMS, explosives, or authorization of the attacks.

**Separately, the DOJ inspector general documented an actual interruption of dissemination.** An FBI detailee drafted a report conveying Mihdhar’s U.S. visa information. A CIA officer entered an instruction to hold it, attributed to the unit’s deputy chief. It was not sent. An internal CIA cable subsequently asserted that the travel documents had been passed to the FBI, but neither inspector general found corroboration for that assertion. The unsent report also surfaced only after earlier document-production efforts had missed it. [DOJ Inspector General](https://oig.justice.gov/sites/default/files/archive/special/s0606/chapter5.htm)

That corroborates **a deliberate decision to pause a particular transmission**, not merely a message lost accidentally. The record does not establish the decision’s ultimate motive or specific advance knowledge of the attack plan. [DOJ Inspector General](https://oig.justice.gov/sites/default/files/archive/special/s0606/chapter5.htm)

There is another concrete reason to audit investigative summaries: a **September 8, 2021 FBI addendum** corrected the claim that Operation Encore had conducted “hundreds” of interviews. A manual re-review found approximately **60 interviews during 2007–2016 concerning its principal subjects and associates**. This is a documented error in the account of investigative scope—not proof of a cover-up, but relevant to evaluating assurances of thoroughness. [FBI](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11-attacks-investigation-and-related-materials/9-11-material-released-in-response-to-executive-order-14040/documents-responsive-to-executive-order-14040-section-2-b-ii-part-01-of-01/at_download/file)

## 3. UMS: support for an intelligence hypothesis, but not yet a demolition role

**Contemporaneous reporting provides a substantive basis for investigating whether UMS was an intelligence front.** In March 2002, *The Forward* reported that an unnamed former senior American intelligence official, who said he received briefings from two law-enforcement officials, described an FBI assessment that UMS was a Mossad front and at least two employees were operatives monitoring local Arabs. Israeli officials denied the allegations, and the relevant U.S. agencies declined comment. [The Forward](https://forward.com/news/325698/spy-rumors-fly-on-gusts-of-truth/)

The limitation is consequential: the same reporting said the men had been cleared of advance knowledge of 9/11. It therefore supports the **possible intelligence-operation component**, while cutting against treating that component as evidence of participation in the attacks. The underlying counterintelligence assessment would be substantially stronger evidence than the anonymous-source account. [The Forward](https://forward.com/news/325698/spy-rumors-fly-on-gusts-of-truth/)

**A released FBI document contains a concrete pre-impact positioning lead.** The October 17, 2001 communication—**303A-NY-C237934, Serial 325, printed page 4**—describes an Exxon surveillance recording showing a *possible* match to the van. Investigators wrote that, **if it was the same vehicle**, its time and position could support a witness account placing individuals at a viewing location before the first strike. [Israel Lobby Archive](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20325%20---%20Section%201%20%281037758%29.pdf)

This matters because it establishes that investigators considered potentially corroborating surveillance evidence, rather than relying exclusively on an eyewitness’s impression. However, the document is explicitly conditional: it does not establish the vehicle match, validate the recording’s clock, or resolve the lead. The decisive material would be the recording and the investigators’ final identification and timing analysis. [Israel Lobby Archive](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20325%20---%20Section%201%20%281037758%29.pdf)

A further concrete locator is the **June 4, 2002 National Security Division communication, Serial 459**, in the same publicly released file collection. Its heavily withheld contents make it a potentially important assessment record, but the redactions cannot legitimately be treated as an admission of intelligence affiliation or foreknowledge. [Israel Lobby Archive](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20459%20---%20Section%201%20%281037765%29.pdf)

For **Classic International Movers**, I did not locate the underlying customer record, identified hijacker, or complete Miami investigative documentation that would independently establish the alleged moving job. Likewise, this search did not locate a verified UMS/Max Movers WTC job or access record. Those remain leads, consistent with the limitations already recorded in your map. Pasted text(20261106-024554) Pasted text(20261106-024554)

## 4. An additional records route: White House material processed in 2026

A **February 10, 2026 National Archives notification** identifies **George W. Bush Presidential Library FOIA case 2025-0218-F**. Its request included “Israeli art students,” “Urban Moving Systems,” and the five detained Israelis, alongside several other names and topics. [archives.gov](https://www.archives.gov/files/foia/pra-notifications/pdf/bush43/rn-plgwb-2026-023.pdf)

The notice reports **13 electronic assets processed**: ten withheld completely, two proposed for partial release, and one for complete release. The proposed material is described as a **security notice and emails concerning press guidance**, drawn from White House ARMS and Exchange email systems. The entry appears on **PDF page 16**. [National Archives](https://www.archives.gov/files/foia/pra-notifications/pdf/bush43/rn-plgwb-2026-023.pdf)

This is a useful, specific archive locator outside the FBI files. **It is not yet corroboration of White House involvement with UMS.** The request bundled multiple subjects, including Rice, Shultz, Chevron, Bechtel, and Iraq-related deliberations; the notice does not identify which search terms matched each record. Nor does a proposed-opening notice verify that the records were ultimately released. [archives.gov](https://www.archives.gov/files/foia/pra-notifications/pdf/bush43/rn-plgwb-2026-023.pdf)

## 5. Physical evidence: affirmative findings worth preserving, with narrower implications

**FEMA documented unusual metallurgical damage.** Appendix C of its 2002 building-performance study examined two severely eroded steel members, one believed to be from WTC 7 and another from a tower. It identified oxidation and sulfidation, including localized intergranular melting in the presumed WTC 7 sample. The authors explicitly left the sulfur’s source and the corrosion rate unresolved, and considered both pre-collapse damage and prolonged heating in the debris as possibilities. They recommended further study. [FEMA](https://www.fema.gov/pdf/library/fema403_apc.pdf)

That is genuine physical evidence supporting targeted questions about chemical processes and follow-up testing. It is **not identification of an incendiary charge**, and the appendix itself did not determine whether the phenomenon preceded the collapse. Describing it as “FEMA found thermite” would substantially overstate the finding. [FEMA](https://www.fema.gov/pdf/library/fema403_apc.pdf)

**There is an affirmative published thermitic-material claim.** Harrit and colleagues’ 2009 paper reported examining red/gray chips from four WTC dust samples and interpreted their results as evidence of active thermitic material. Unlike an argument from appearance alone, this is a laboratory claim with described analytical methods. [benthamopenarchives.com](https://benthamopenarchives.com/abstract.php?ArticleCode=TOCPJ-2-7)

The important qualification is that James Millette’s 2012 laboratory investigation of visually similar chips from **different dust samples** identified epoxy coating constituents and did not identify elemental aluminum, reaching a non-thermite interpretation. Because he did not examine Harrit’s actual specimens, it is not a direct same-specimen replication. It nevertheless demonstrates why appearance and elemental overlap alone cannot settle identification. The evidentiary issue is distinguishing the particular specimens, establishing provenance, and obtaining independent analysis—not simply counting papers on either side. [ANETA](https://aneta.org/911experiments_com/Millette/paper/index.htm)

**The UAF study supplies a specific alternative structural analysis.** Its researchers concluded that fire did not cause WTC 7’s collapse and that the observed behavior involved near-simultaneous failure of all columns. The university hosts the report, simulations, and downloadable computational data; the project was funded by Architects & Engineers for 9/11 Truth. This supports the existence of an explicit, inspectable engineering challenge to NIST—not identification of an explosive mechanism or responsible personnel. A modeled failure pattern does not, by itself, establish what physically produced it. [INE University of Alaska Fairbanks](https://ine.uaf.edu/wtc7)

Two NIST acknowledgments also remain relevant. NIST recognizes a **2.25-second free-fall interval of WTC 7’s north face**, while explaining it through prior structural failure. Separately, it states that it **did not test the towers’ steel for explosive or thermite residues**. Thus, its rejection of demolition should not be represented as a negative residue-test result. Neither acknowledgment independently establishes demolition. [NIST](https://www.nist.gov/world-trade-center-investigation/study-faqs/wtc-7-investigation)

None of these physical sources identifies UMS. Even a successful challenge to a collapse model would leave the perpetrator-attribution problem unresolved.

## 6. Iraq: strong contemporaneous evidence of an existing objective and subsequent policy use

**A July 27, 2001 Rumsfeld memorandum to Condoleezza Rice predates the attacks.** Its working paper discusses Iraq policy alternatives, including regime change, and argues that Saddam’s removal would improve the United States’ regional position. Because it also discusses other options, it establishes that regime change was under active consideration—not that a particular invasion or provocation had already been decided. [National Security Archive](https://nsarchive2.gwu.edu/NSAEBB/NSAEBB326/doc06.pdf)

**Stephen Cambone’s September 11 notes record Rumsfeld considering Iraq immediately after the attacks.** They include the question of striking Saddam Hussein alongside bin Laden and language about sweeping in matters both related and unrelated. This is contemporaneous documentary support for the proposition that officials considered using the attack’s aftermath to pursue objectives beyond the identified perpetrators. [National Security Archive](https://nsarchive2.gwu.edu/NSAEBB/NSAEBB326/doc07.pdf)

**The Defense Department inspector general later documented a relevant institutional mechanism.** In 2007 testimony, the acting inspector general said the policy office produced and disseminated alternative assessments of Iraq–al-Qaeda relations that diverged from intelligence-community conclusions without adequately communicating those differences. He characterized the activity as inappropriate, though not illegal or unauthorized. This supports scrutiny of how policy preferences shaped the presentation of intelligence. [GovInfo](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/CHRG-110shrg35438/html/CHRG-110shrg35438.htm)

For the commercial-benefit component, GAO documented the sole-source Iraq oil-infrastructure contract and examined its justification. But it found that this award complied with the applicable competition exception. The record supports the existence of concentrated commercial opportunity; it does not independently establish corrupt procurement, advance knowledge of 9/11, or participation in the attacks. [GAO](https://www.gao.gov/assets/a242676.html)

**The supported inference is “existing objectives influenced the exploitation and interpretation of the aftermath.” The stronger claim—“the attacks were organized to create that opportunity”—still requires pre-attack operational evidence connecting those objectives to the perpetrators.**

## What this changes in the evidence map

The most consequential additions are **the FBI’s Bayoumi intelligence-affiliation memorandum, the specific recruitment allegations in Canestraro’s declaration, the newly public Bayoumi–Awlaki relationship evidence, and the documented corrections and restrictions in investigative accounts**. They provide concrete reasons to investigate intelligence relationships and information control, rather than relying solely on institutional motive. [HSGAC](https://www.hsgac.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023-09-10-Blumenthal-and-Johnson-Letter-to-DOJ-and-FBI-re-Sept.-11.pdf)

For the UMS hypothesis specifically, the greatest evidentiary value would come from resolving the **conditional surveillance identification and underlying counterintelligence assessment**, then establishing an actual WTC access-and-materials connection. More general evidence of secrecy, intelligence activity, or geopolitical benefit cannot substitute for that missing connection. [Israel Lobby Archive](https://www.israellobby.org/urbanmoving/1169683-001%20---%20303A-NY-C237934-Serial%20325%20---%20Section%201%20%281037758%29.pdf) Pasted text(20261106-024554)
